\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T^{k}_{1,w}||_{r+\alpha}&\lesssim||[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j},\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}]T^{k}_{1,w}||_{r+\alpha}+||(z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})\cdot\nabla\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T^{k}_{1,w}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}T^{k}_{1,w}||_{r+\alpha}\\
&\lesssim\underbrace{||z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{r+1+\alpha}||T^{k}_{1,w}||_{\alpha}+||z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{1+\alpha}||T^{k}_{1,w}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\ell^{-2\alpha}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q}^{1/2}\delta_{q+1}}\\
&+\underbrace{||z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}||T^{k}_{1,w}||_{1+\alpha}+||z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{0}||T^{k}_{1,w}||_{r+1+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-2\alpha}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\delta_{q+1}}\\
&+\underbrace{||[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\mathcal{L}_{\xi^{g}}^{k}(\partial_{t}+\mathcal{L}_{v_{\ell,j}})\Theta_{j}^{g}]\times\nabla v_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}}+\underbrace{||\mathcal{L}_{\xi^{g}}^{k}(\partial_{t}+\mathcal{L}_{v_{\ell,j}})\Theta_{j}^{g}\times\nabla\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}v_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}}\\
&+\underbrace{||\mathcal{L}_{\xi^{g}}^{k}(\partial_{t}+\mathcal{L}_{v_{\ell,j}})\Theta_{j}^{g}\times(\mathrm{D}z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})^{\top}(\mathrm{D}v_{\ell,j})^{\top}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\mathcal{T}^{k}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q}^{1/2}\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}}\\
&\lesssim(C)^{k}\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r+k-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\\
\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-m_{0}-1
and
0\leq r+k\leq\bar{N}-1
. Moreover,

\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T^{k}_{2,w}||_{r+\alpha}&\lesssim||[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j},\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}]T^{k}_{2,w}||_{r+\alpha}+||(z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})\cdot\nabla\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T^{k}_{2,w}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}T^{k}_{2,w}||_{r+\alpha}\\
&\lesssim\underbrace{||z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{r+1+\alpha}||T^{k}_{2,w}||_{\alpha}+||z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{1+\alpha}||T^{k}_{2,w}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}\ell^{-2\alpha}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}}\\
&+\underbrace{||z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}||T^{k}_{2,w}||_{1+\alpha}+||z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{0}||T^{k}_{2,w}||_{r+1+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{2m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-2\alpha}\delta_{q+1}}\\
&+\underbrace{||[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\mathcal{L}_{\xi^{g}}^{k}\mathcal{L}_{v_{q}-v_{\ell,j}}\Theta_{j}^{g}]\times\nabla v_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}}+\underbrace{||\mathcal{L}_{\xi^{g}}^{k}\mathcal{L}_{v_{q}-v_{\ell,j}}\Theta_{j}^{g}\times\nabla\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}v_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}}\\
&+\underbrace{||\mathcal{L}_{\xi^{g}}^{k}\mathcal{L}_{v_{q}-v_{\ell,j}}\Theta_{j}^{g}\times(\mathrm{D}z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})^{\top}(\mathrm{D}v_{\ell,j})^{\top}||_{r+\alpha}}_{\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}}\\
&\lesssim(C)^{k}\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{\mathcal{A}}(r+k)\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r+k\leq N-m_{0}-3
.

Similarly, one can show:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{1,b}^{k}||_{r+\alpha}&\lesssim(C)^{k}\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r+k-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-m_{0}-1,\ 0\leq r+k\leq\bar{N}-1,\\
||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{2,b}^{k}||_{r+\alpha}&\lesssim(C)^{k}\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{\mathcal{A}}(r+k)\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r+k\leq N-m_{0}-3.\\
\end{split}

The implicit constants and
C
in the above depend on
r
and the other parameters, but not on
a,\ k
.

Conclusion.
To complete the proof we need to sum up these estimates over
k
, we will split the sum according to the definitions of the loss functions
\lambda_{q}^{[r+k-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}},\ L_{\mathcal{A}}(r+k)
, see (
2.42
), and the number of derivatives
r
to isolate the bad range in which the losses
\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}},\ \bar{L}
appears. We proceed as in the proof of the Alfvén transport bound in Lemma
2.15
, see (
2.52
).

Collecting the bounds above together with the fact that at most two
\Theta_{j}^{g}
are non-zero at the same time, we get:

(2.68)

\begin{split}||R^{g,na}||_{r+\alpha}&\leq\sum_{k=1}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{1}{(k+1)!}\left[||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{1,2}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{2,w}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{1,b}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{2,b}||_{r+\alpha}\right]\\
&\lesssim\sum_{k=1}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{(C)^{k}}{(k+1)!}\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r+k-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{(C)^{k}}{(k+1)!}\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r+k-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}}\frac{\left(C\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k}}{(k+1)!}+\sum_{k=[\underline{r}-r]^{+}+1}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{\left(C\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k}}{(k+1)!}\right]\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\left[\sum_{k=0}^{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}-1}\frac{\left(C\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k}}{(k+1)!}+\sum_{k=[\underline{r}-r]^{+}}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{\left(C\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k}}{(k+1)!}\right]\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\mathcal{T}_{g}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\left[1_{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}\geq 1}+1_{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}+1\leq k_{0}^{g}}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\left(\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}}\right]\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\left[1_{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}\geq 1}+1_{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}\leq k_{0}^{g}}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\left(\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{[\underline{r}-r]^{+}}\right]\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-k_{0}^{g}-m_{0}-2
. The implicit constant depends on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
. Here we used that
(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\leq\lambda_{q}/\lambda_{q+1}\leq\mathcal{T}_{g}
, see (
1.24
).

We proceed to the Alfvén transport estimate.

\begin{split}&||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,na}||_{r+\alpha}\\
&\leq\sum_{k=1}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{1}{(k+1)!}\left[||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{1,2}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{2,w}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{1,b}||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}T_{2,b}||_{r+\alpha}\right]\\
\\
&\lesssim\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{(C)^{k}}{(k+1)!}\left[\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\right]}_{S_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{k=0}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{(C)^{k}}{(k+1)!}\left[\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{\mathcal{A}}(r+k)\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\right]}_{S_{2}}\\
\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-k_{0}^{g}-m_{0}-3
.

We can bound
S_{1}
as in (
2.68
), and deduce:

S_{1}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq\bar{N}-k_{0}^{g}=N

To deal with the loss function
L_{\mathcal{A}}
in
S_{2}
, we proceed as in (
2.67
); we omit the details.

\begin{split}S_{2}&=\lambda_{q}^{r}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\sum_{k=0}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{(C)^{k}}{(k+1)!}\left[L_{\mathcal{A}}(r+k)\mathcal{T}^{k}_{g}\right]\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\Biggr[\underbrace{\sum_{k=0}^{[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}-1}\frac{\left(C\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k}}{(k+1)!}}_{\lesssim 1_{[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}\geq 1}\mathcal{T}_{g}}+\bar{L}\underbrace{\sum_{k=[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}}^{k_{0}^{g}}\frac{\left(C\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k}}{(k+1)!}}_{\lesssim 1_{[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}\leq k_{0}^{g}}\left(\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}}}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\Biggr]\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\mathcal{T}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\left[1_{[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}\geq 1}+1_{[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}\leq k_{0}^{g}}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\left(\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{[\underline{r}-1-r]^{+}}\bar{L}\right]\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-k_{0}^{g}-m_{0}-3
.

We conclude that:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,na}||_{r+\alpha}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\mathcal{T}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-k_{0}^{g}-m_{0}-3
, where we used that
(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\leq\lambda_{q}/\lambda_{q+1}\leq\mathcal{T}_{g}
, see (
1.24
). The implicit constant depends on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

The proof of the Lemma is complete upon noticing that according to our choice of parameters in
1.5
, see (
1.31
), we have:

N-k_{0}^{g}-m_{0}-2\geq M

and thus the bounds hold for
0\leq r\leq M
and
0\leq r\leq M-1
respectively.
∎

Lemma 2.19

(Estimates on the Remainder Error)
.

Let
\underline{r}=M-m_{0}-6
. Under the choice of parameters
1.5
, the following bounds hold:

\begin{split}||R^{g,rem}||_{r+\alpha}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }0\leq r\leq N-(k_{0}^{g}+1)-m_{0}-3,\\
||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,rem}||_{r+\alpha}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-(k_{0}^{g}+1)-m_{0}-4.\end{split}

The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
. In particular, the bounds hold for
0\leq r\leq M
and
0\leq r\leq M-1
, respectively.

Proof of Lemma
2.19
.

The key ingredient in the proof is the fact that from the choice of parameters in
1.5
, see (
1.24
), we have:

\left(\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k_{0}^{g}}\leq\delta_{q}.

According to the estimates in Lemmas
2.13
with
k_{0}=k_{0}^{g}
and
2.14
and Proposition
B.7
to deal with
\mathcal{R}\mathrm{curl}
and
\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}
, we can bound:

\begin{split}||R^{g,rem}||_{r+\alpha}&\leq||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{curl}[\partial_{t}\theta_{w}]||_{r+\alpha}+||\mathcal{R}\mathrm{div}\left[v_{q}\otimes r_{w}+r_{w}\otimes v_{q}-B_{q}\otimes r_{b}-r_{b}\otimes B_{q}\right]||_{r+\alpha}\\
&\lesssim||\partial_{t}\theta_{w}||_{r+\alpha}+||v_{q}\otimes r_{w}+r_{w}\otimes v_{q}-B_{q}\otimes r_{b}-r_{b}\otimes B_{q}||_{r+\alpha}\\
&\lesssim||\partial_{t}\theta_{w}||_{r+\alpha}+||v_{q}||_{r+\alpha}||\theta_{w}||_{1}+||v_{q}||_{0}||\theta_{w}||_{r+1+\alpha}+||B_{q}||_{r+\alpha}||\theta_{b}||_{1}+||B_{q}||_{0}||\theta_{b}||_{r+1+\alpha}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\left(\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k_{0}^{g}+1}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\leq\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\tau^{a}/\tau^{c}\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-(k_{0}^{g}+1)-m_{0}-3
.

We estimate the transport derivatives using only first- and second-order pure time derivatives. We obtain:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,rem}||_{r+\alpha}&\leq||\partial_{t}R^{g,rem}||_{r+\alpha}+||z^{\pm}_{q}\cdot\nabla R^{g,rem}||_{r+\alpha}\\
&\lesssim||\partial_{t}^{2}\theta_{w}||_{r+\alpha}+||[\partial_{t}v_{q}\otimes\mathrm{curl}\ \theta_{w}]^{sym}||_{r+\alpha}+||[v_{q}\otimes\mathrm{curl}\ \partial_{t}\theta_{w}]^{sym}||_{r+\alpha}\\
&+||[\partial_{t}B_{q}\otimes\mathrm{curl}\ \theta_{b}]^{sym}||_{r+\alpha}+||[B_{q}\otimes\mathrm{curl}\ \partial_{t}\theta_{b}]^{sym}||_{r+\alpha}\\
&+||z^{\pm}_{q}||_{r+\alpha}||R^{g,rem}||_{1}+||z^{\pm}_{q}||_{0}||R^{g,rem}||_{r+\alpha+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+2}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\left(\lambda_{q}^{(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}\right)^{k_{0}^{g}+1}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\leq\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-(k_{0}^{g}+1)-m_{0}-4
.

Given our choice of parameters in
1.5
, see (
1.31
), the bound above hold in particular for
0\leq r\leq M
and
0\leq r\leq M-1
respectively. Moreover, the implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.
∎

Lemma 2.20

(Estimates on the Mollification Error)
.

Let
\underline{r}=M-m_{0}-6
. Under the choice of parameters in
1.5
, the following bounds hold:

\begin{split}||R^{g,mo}||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M,\\
||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,mo}||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}1/\tau^{c}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M-1.\end{split}

The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

Proof of Lemma
2.20
.

During the proof, we will need the estimates in Lemma
2.14
. These come with the loss function:

L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)=1_{r\leq\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1}+1_{r\geq\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q}}\right)^{r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)}=\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}.

We will structure the estimates on
R_{q}-R_{\ell},\ v_{q}-v_{\ell},\ B_{q}-B_{\ell}
in the same way. We now recall the definition of
R^{g,mo}
and split it in the following way:

\begin{split}R^{g,mo}&=\sum_{j}[\underbrace{(v_{q}-v_{\ell,j})\otimes(w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h})}_{T_{1,w}}]^{sym}-[\underbrace{(B_{q}-B_{\ell,j})\otimes(b_{j}^{g,p}+b_{j}^{h})}_{T_{1,b}}]^{sym}+\underbrace{R_{q}-R_{\ell}}_{T_{2}},\\
\end{split}

we omit the dependence on
j
of
T_{1,w},\ T_{1,b}
.

Estimates on
T_{2}
.
For
0\leq r\leq M-m_{0}
, the required bounds are contained in Lemmas
2.1
,
2.3
. For
M-m_{0}\leq r\leq M
, we bound each term separately and trade a bad derivative for a good one to retain the smallness. We write this in a compact form as:

\begin{split}||R_{q}-R_{\ell}||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q+1}\left[1_{r\leq M-m_{0}}+1_{r\geq M-m_{0}+1}\frac{\lambda_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q}}\frac{\lambda_{q}}{\lambda_{q+1}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}}\right]\\
&\leq\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(M-m_{0})]^{+}(b-1)}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M,\\
||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}(R_{q}-R_{\ell})||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\delta_{q+1}\left[1_{r\leq M-m_{0}-1}+1_{r\geq M-m_{0}}\frac{\lambda_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q}}\frac{\lambda_{q}}{\lambda_{q+1}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}}\right]\\
&\leq\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(M-m_{0}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M-1.\end{split}

Estimates on
T_{1,w}
.
From the bounds in Lemmas
2.1
,
2.3
,
2.15
,
2.16
, we deduce:

\begin{split}||T_{1,w}||_{r}&\lesssim(||v_{q}-v_{\ell}||_{r}+||v_{\ell}-v_{\ell,j}||_{r})||w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h}||_{0}+(||v_{q}-v_{\ell}||_{0}+||v_{\ell}-v_{\ell,j}||_{0})||w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-m_{0}
.

We move to the Alfvén transport estimates. We first rewrite:

\begin{split}\mathcal{A}^{\pm}T_{1,w}&=\left[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}(v_{q}-v_{\ell})+(z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})\cdot\nabla(v_{\ell}-v_{\ell,j})+\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(v_{\ell}-v_{\ell,j})\right]\otimes(w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h})\\
&+(v_{q}-v_{\ell,j})\otimes\left[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h})+(z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})\cdot\nabla(w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h})\right]\\
\end{split}

Reasoning as in
T_{2}
, using the bounds in Lemma
2.1
and in the Iterative Assumptions (
1.16
), we first write for
0\leq r\leq M-1
:

\begin{split}||[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}(v_{q}-v_{\ell})||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}\left[1_{r\leq M-m_{0}-1}+1_{r\geq M-m_{0}}\frac{\lambda_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q}}\frac{\lambda_{q}}{\lambda_{q+1}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}}\right]\\
&\leq\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(M-m_{0}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\frac{\lambda_{q}^{2}\delta_{q}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\end{split}

and with the additional use of the bounds in Lemmas
2.3
,
2.12
,
2.14
, we deduce:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}T_{1,w}||_{r}&\lesssim\left\|\left[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}(v_{q}-v_{\ell})+(z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})\cdot\nabla(v_{\ell}-v_{\ell,j})+\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(v_{\ell}-v_{\ell,j})\right]\otimes(w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h})\right\|_{r}\\
&+\left\|(v_{q}-v_{\ell,j})\otimes\left[\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h})+(z^{\pm}_{q}-z^{\pm}_{\ell,j})\cdot\nabla(w_{j}^{g,p}+w_{j}^{h})\right]\right\|_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(M-m_{0}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\frac{\lambda_{q}^{2}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\mathcal{T}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(1/\tau^{c})\delta_{q}\\
\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}1/\tau^{c}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq M-1
. Here we used the definition of
\mathcal{T}_{g}=\lambda_{q}^{2}\tau^{a}\tau^{c}\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}
and the fact that
M-1\leq N-k_{0}^{g}-m_{0}-3
, see (
1.31
).

Using the fact that at most two labels
j
are active at each time and the fact that
T_{1,b}
enjoys the same bounds as
T_{1,w}
, we conclude that:

\begin{split}||R^{g,mo}||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(M-m_{0})]^{+}(b-1)}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M,\\
||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,mo}||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}1/\tau^{c}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(M-m_{0}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}1/\tau^{c}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M-1.\\
\end{split}

The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.
∎

Lemma 2.21

(Estimates on the Quadratic Error)
.

Let
\underline{r}=M-m_{0}-6
. Under the choice of parameters in
1.5
, the following bounds hold:

\begin{split}||R^{g,qua}||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\left(\lambda_{q}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\right)^{2}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M,\\
||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,qua}||_{r}&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}(1/\tau^{a})\left(\lambda_{q}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\right)^{2}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M-1.\\
\end{split}

The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

The estimates follow immediately from its definition in (
2.55
), the decomposition in (
2.35
) and the bounds Lemmas
2.15
,
2.16
. We also remark that:

\left(\lambda_{q}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\right)^{2}\leq(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})^{2}(\delta_{q+1}/\delta_{q})\delta_{q+1}.

2.6.
Checkpoint
(v_{q},\ B_{q},\ R_{q})\leadsto(\tilde{v}_{q},\ \tilde{B}_{q},\ \tilde{R}_{q})

Before proceeding with the Nash stage, we summarise the work and estimates shown so far. We denote the objects resulting from the Galbrun stage either with a superscript
g
or with a tilde. The vector fields and pressure are perturbed in the following way:

(\tilde{v}_{q},\ \tilde{B}_{q},\ \tilde{p}_{q})=(v_{q}+w^{g},\ B_{q}+b^{g},\ p_{q}+\pi^{g})

while the right-hand side of (
1.15
) is now:

\tilde{R}_{q}:=R^{g}+\sum_{I}g_{I}^{2}A_{I}.

According to the decomposition (
1.19
), with
(w^{p},b^{p},\pi^{p})
and
\tilde{A}_{I}
equal to zero, and the fact that our way of constructing the perturbation ensures that the Faraday-Ohm system (
1.1
) is solved exactly, we have:

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}\tilde{v}_{q}+\tilde{v}_{q}\cdot\nabla\tilde{v}_{q}-\tilde{B}_{q}\cdot\nabla\tilde{B}_{q}+\nabla\tilde{p}_{q}=\mathrm{div}\ \tilde{R}_{q},\\
\partial_{t}\tilde{B}_{q}+\mathrm{curl}[\tilde{B}_{q}\times\tilde{v}_{q}]=0,\\
\mathrm{div}\ \tilde{v}_{q}=\mathrm{div}\ \tilde{B}_{q}=0.\\
\end{cases}

We now gather the estimates on these intermediate objects.

Proposition 2.22

(Galbrun Stage)
.

Set
\underline{r}=M-m_{0}-6
and let
L_{g,\mathcal{A}}:\mathbb{N}_{\geq 0}\to\mathbb{R}_{\geq 1}
be the admissible loss function from Lemma
2.15
. The following bounds hold:

(2.69a)

\displaystyle|\tilde{v}_{q}|,\ |\tilde{B}_{q}|\leq C_{0}(1-3\delta_{q+1}^{1/2})\ \text{ and }\ |\tilde{B}_{q}|\geq c_{0}(1+2\delta_{q+1}^{1/2})\ \text{ everywhere on }\ \mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R},

(2.69b)

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}^{j}\tilde{v}_{q}||_{r},\ ||\partial_{t}^{j}\tilde{B}_{q}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ j=0,1,2,\ \&\ 0\leq r\leq N-j\ \&\ (j,r)\neq(0,0),

(2.69c)

\displaystyle||\tilde{p}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ 1\leq r\leq M,

(2.69d)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{v}_{q}||_{r},\ ||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{B}_{q}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-1,

(2.69e)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{p}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 1\leq r\leq M-1,

(2.69f)

\displaystyle||R^{g}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)}\ell^{-\alpha}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M,

(2.69g)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}R^{g}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M-1.

Remark 11

(New Pressure Bound)
.

Here, we opt for a simplified notation and very suboptimal estimates. Note, however, the loss in the transport bound for the pressure, when compared to the one in (
1.16
), this is because
\tilde{R}_{q}
contains
\sum_{I}g_{I}^{2}A_{I}
, which has no additional smallness, and
\mathrm{div}\mathrm{div}\sum_{I}g_{I}^{2}A_{I}\neq 0
, so the pressure sees the fast oscillations we introduced in the Galbrun Stage. In the vector fields’ bounds, the additional smallness of the Galbrun perturbation compensates for this fact.

Remark 12

(Total Good Derivative Loss
\underline{r}
)
.

We start with
M
and then lose:

•

m_{0}
in the deep mollification.

•

1 in the local recorrection as we solve a first-order (non-linear) PDE.

•

1 for the construction of the charts in the Grönwall estimate for the flow.

•

2 in Galbrun’s equation as it is of second-order.

•

1 in the correction terms of the Lie series
\mathcal{L}_{v_{q}-v_{\ell}}\Theta_{j}^{g}
.

•

1 passing from
\Theta_{j}^{g}\leadsto\xi_{j}^{g}
.

In the Alfvén transport and Reynold stress estimates, we lose an additional
k_{0}^{g}
due to the Lie-Taylor expansion needed to read the geometric properties.

Proof of Proposition
2.22
.

We deal with the vector fields and the pressure first, and gather the bounds for
R^{g}
at the end.

Recap vector fields.
We will show the computations only for the velocity field; the bounds for the magnetic field follow similarly. The estimates in Lemmas
2.15
and
2.16
give:

\begin{split}||\partial_{t}^{j}w^{g}||_{r}&\leq||\partial_{t}^{j}\bar{w}^{g}||_{r}+||\partial_{t}^{j}\mathring{w}^{g}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-j
, where we used that:

\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}=\lambda_{q+1}^{\alpha}\mathcal{T}_{g}\delta_{q}^{1/2}>\mathcal{T}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}.

Moreover,

\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}w^{g}||_{r}&\leq||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\bar{w}^{g}||_{r}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\mathring{w}^{g}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)\mathcal{T}_{g}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(1/\tau^{c})\delta_{q}^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)\ell^{-\alpha}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}.\end{split}

From this, we also deduce:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}w^{g}||_{r}&\leq||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}w^{g}||_{r}+||(w^{g}\pm b^{g})\cdot\nabla w^{g}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)\ell^{-\alpha}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\lambda_{q}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1})^{2}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)\ell^{-\alpha}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-1
. The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the parameters, but not on
a
.

Given our Iterative Assumptions (
1.16
) we conclude, for
j=0,1,2
,
0\leq r\leq N-j
and
(j,r)\neq(0,0)
, that:

\begin{split}||\partial_{t}^{j}\tilde{v}_{q}||_{r}&\leq||\partial_{t}^{j}v_{q}||_{r}+||\partial_{t}^{j}w^{g}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\delta_{q}^{1/2}+\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\end{split}

For
r=j=0
, we have for some constant
C
depending on the parameters but not on
a
:

||\tilde{v}_{q}||_{0}\leq||v_{q}||_{0}+||w^{g}||_{0}\leq C_{0}(1-\delta_{q}^{1/2})+C\lambda_{q}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\leq C_{0}(1-3\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}),

where the last inequality follows by choosing
a
sufficiently large, given all the other parameters, so that:

\delta_{q}^{1/2}/\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\geq 3+C/C_{0}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}/\delta_{q}^{1/2}).

We now check the non-vanishing condition. For some constant
C
depending on the parameters but not on
a
, we have:

\begin{split}|\tilde{B}_{q}|\geq|B_{q}|-|b^{g}|\geq c_{0}(1+\delta_{q}^{1/2})-C\lambda_{q}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\geq c_{0}(1+2\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}),\end{split}

where the last inequality follows again by choosing
a
large enough.

Note that by bridging the Alfvén transport estimate with the pure time derivative bound, we can write:

||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}v_{q}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}\left[1_{r\leq M-1}+\bar{L}1_{r\geq M}\right]\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-1

where
\bar{L}=\delta_{q}^{1/2}
. By definition, see (
2.43a
), we have:

1_{r\leq M-1}+\bar{L}1_{r\geq M}\leq L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)

and thus

\begin{split}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{v}_{q}||_{r}&\leq||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}v_{q}||_{r}+||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}w^{g}||_{r}+||(w^{g}\pm b^{g})\cdot\nabla v_{q}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}\left[1_{r\leq M-1}+\bar{L}1_{r\geq M}\right]+\lambda_{q}^{r}L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)\ell^{-\alpha}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\leq\lambda_{q}^{r+1}L_{g,\mathcal{A}}(r)\delta_{q}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq N-1
. The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
. Here we used that
2\beta\geq\gamma_{CZ}
, see (
1.32
).

Recap pressure.
The claimed estimates follow directly from the ones in the Iterative Assumptions (
1.16
) and Lemma
2.9
.

(2.70)

\begin{split}||\tilde{p}_{q}||_{r}&\leq||p_{q}||_{r}+||\pi^{g}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\delta_{q}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq M
. Moreover,

\begin{split}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{p}_{q}||_{r}&\lesssim||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}p_{q}||_{r}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}\pi^{g}||_{r}+||w^{g}\pm b^{g}||_{r}||p_{q}+\pi^{g}||_{1}+||w^{g}\pm b^{g}||_{0}||p_{q}+\pi^{g}||_{r+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\delta_{q}^{3/2}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r+2}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}\tau^{a}\delta_{q}\delta_{q+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq M-1
, where we used that thanks to our choice of parameters in (
1.5
), see (
1.34
), we have:

\gamma_{a}=\frac{1}{2}(1-\beta)\text{ and }\beta<1/5\Longrightarrow 2\beta\leq\gamma_{a}\Longrightarrow\tau^{c}/\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}/\delta_{q}\geq 1\Longrightarrow\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}\geq\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{3/2}.

Recap Reynold stress.
Collecting the bounds from Lemmas
2.10
,
2.17
,
2.18
,
2.19
,
2.20
,
2.21
, we deduce:

\begin{split}||R^{g}||_{r}&\leq||R^{g,tr}||_{r}+||R^{cut}||_{r}+||R^{g,moll}||_{r}+||R^{g,na}||_{r}+||R^{g,rem}||_{r}+||R^{g,qua}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)}\mathcal{T}_{g}\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})^{2}(\delta_{q+1}/\delta_{q})\delta_{q+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g})]^{+}(b-1)}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\\
\end{split}

for
0\leq r\leq M
. The implicit constant depends on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
. Here we used that:

\frac{\lambda_{q}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\leq\mathcal{T}^{g}=\lambda_{q}^{2}\tau^{c}\tau^{a}\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}\leq(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\lambda_{q+1}^{-\alpha}(\delta_{q+1}/\delta_{q})\leq(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})

see (
1.24
), (
2.38
).

From the same Lemmas, we also get:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g}||_{r}&\leq||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,tr}||_{r}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{cut}||_{r}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,na}||_{r}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,moll}||_{r}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,rem}||_{r}+||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g,qua}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\mathcal{T}_{g}(1/\tau^{a})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\mathcal{T}_{g}(1/\tau^{c})\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}1/\tau^{c}\frac{\lambda_{q}\delta_{q+1}}{\lambda_{q+1}}\\
&+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}(1/\tau^{c})(\tau^{a}/\tau^{c})(\delta_{q+1}/\delta_{q})\delta_{q+1}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{c})\delta_{q+1}\end{split}

and we conclude that:

||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}R^{g}||_{r}\leq||\mathcal{A}^{\pm}R^{g}||_{r}+||(w^{g}\pm b^{g})\cdot\nabla R^{g}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-k_{0}^{g}-1)]^{+}(b-1)}1/\tau^{c}\ell^{-\alpha}\delta_{q+1}

for
0\leq r\leq M-1
. The implicit constant depends on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.
∎

3.
Nash Stage

We now construct the main part of the perturbation. This will erase the ‘well-prepared’ Reynold stress from the Galbrun stage. Many of the techniques and ideas have been discussed in previous sections; we will provide only brief summaries.

3.1.
Space Mollification
(\tilde{v}_{q},\ \tilde{B}_{q})\leadsto(\tilde{v}_{\ell,j},\ \tilde{B}_{\ell,j})

The vector fields
(\tilde{B}_{q},\ \tilde{v}_{q})
resulting from the Galbrun stage contain terms which are not mollified, and thus we need to perform a second mollification to avoid a possible loss of derivatives. As before, since this procedure breaks the commutativity of the Alfvén transport operators, we will construct a secondary local recorrection. The ideas are the same as in Section
2.1
, and we omit the proofs.

Recall the notation from Proposition
2.22
, we now set:

\begin{cases}\tilde{v}_{\ell}=(\tilde{v}_{q})_{\ell},\\
\tilde{B}_{\ell}=(\tilde{B}_{q})_{\ell},\\
\tilde{z}^{\pm}_{\ell}=\tilde{v}_{\ell}\pm\tilde{B}_{\ell}\end{cases}\text{ and }\begin{cases}\tilde{R}_{\ell}=(\tilde{R}_{q})_{\ell},\\
\tilde{M}_{\ell}=(\tilde{B}_{\ell}\times\tilde{v}_{\ell})-(\tilde{B}_{q}\times\tilde{v}_{q})_{\ell},\\
\tilde{R}_{\ell}^{c}=\left(\tilde{v}_{\ell}\otimes\tilde{v}_{\ell}-\tilde{B}_{\ell}\otimes\tilde{B}_{\ell}\right)-\left(\tilde{v}_{q}\otimes\tilde{v}_{q}-\tilde{B}_{q}\otimes\tilde{B}_{q}\right)_{\ell}\\
\end{cases}

together with Alfvén transport operators:

(3.1)

\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}=\partial_{t}+\tilde{z}^{\pm}_{q}\cdot\nabla,\ \mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell}=\partial_{t}+\tilde{z}^{\pm}_{\ell}\cdot\nabla,\ \mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}=\partial_{t}+\tilde{z}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\cdot\nabla.

We gather the estimates concerning the mollification in the following lemma.

Lemma 3.1

(Mollification Estimates)
.

Let
r\geq 0
be an integer and
\underline{r}=M-m_{0}-k_{0}^{g}-6
. We have:

(3.2a)

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}^{j}\tilde{v}_{\ell}||_{r},||\partial_{t}^{j}\tilde{B}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }j=0,1,2\ \&\ (r,j)\neq(0,0),

(3.2b)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{v}_{\ell}||_{r},\ ||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{B}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-1,

(3.2c)

\displaystyle||\tilde{p}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1,

(3.2d)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{p}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 1\leq r\leq N-1,

(3.2e)

\displaystyle||\tilde{R}_{\ell}||\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1},

(3.2f)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}\tilde{R}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}1/\tau^{a}\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-1.

Moreover, let
L_{p,\mathcal{A}}:\mathbb{N}_{\geq 0}\to\mathbb{R}_{\geq 1}
be the admissible loss function in (
3.42
), the following error bounds hold:

(3.3a)

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}^{j}(\tilde{v}_{q}-\tilde{v}_{\ell})||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-(\underline{r}-m_{0})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }j=0,1,2,\ \&\ 0\leq r\leq N-m_{0}-j,

(3.3b)

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}^{j}(\tilde{B}_{q}-\tilde{B}_{\ell})||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-(\underline{r}-m_{0})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }j=0,1,2,\ \&\ 0\leq r\leq N-m_{0}-j,

(3.3c)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}(\tilde{v}_{q}-\tilde{v}_{\ell})||_{r},||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}(\tilde{B}_{q}-\tilde{B}_{\ell})||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}L_{p,\mathcal{A}}(r)(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-m_{0}-1,

(3.3d)

\displaystyle||\tilde{p}_{q}-\tilde{p}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-m_{0})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ 1\leq r\leq M-m_{0},

(3.3e)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}(\tilde{p}_{q}-\tilde{p}_{\ell})||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-m_{0}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq M-m_{0}-1,

(3.3f)

\displaystyle||\tilde{R}_{q}-\tilde{R}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq\underline{r}-m_{0},

(3.3g)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}(\tilde{R}_{q}-\tilde{R}_{\ell})||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq\underline{r}-m_{0}-1.

Finally, we have the following bounds for the forcings:

(3.4)

\begin{split}&||\tilde{R}_{\ell}^{c}||_{r},\ ||\tilde{M}_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-m_{0})]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q},\\
&||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell}\tilde{R}_{\ell}^{c}||_{r},\ ||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell}M_{\ell}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-m_{0}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}^{3/2}.\\
\end{split}

The implicit constants depend on
r
, all the parameters, and the specific choice of the mollification kernel, but not on
a
.

The bounds follow immediately from the ones in Propositions
2.22
and
B.6
. It is important to remark here that since we proved ‘good properties’ up to
r=\underline{r}
derivatives, after mollification, we retain them for all
r
upon paying
\ell^{-1}
for each additional derivative.

We are now ready to construct the corrections. Let
j
be a fixed index of the time partition
\{\eta_{j}\}_{j}
from Subsection
2.1
. We now solve on
\mathbb{T}^{3}\times(t_{j}-\tau^{c},t_{j}+\tau^{c})
the full relaxed MHD system:

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}+\mathrm{div}[\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}\otimes\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}-\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}\otimes\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}]+\nabla\tilde{p}_{\ell,j}=\mathrm{div}\ \tilde{R}_{\ell},\\
\partial_{t}\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}+\mathrm{curl}[\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}\times\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}]=0,\\
\mathrm{div}\ \tilde{B}_{\ell,j}=\mathrm{div}\ \tilde{v}_{\ell,j}=0,\\
(\tilde{v}_{\ell,j},\tilde{B}_{\ell,j})|_{t=t_{j}}=(\tilde{v}_{\ell},\tilde{B}_{\ell})|_{t=t_{j}}.\end{cases}

Existence and uniqueness follow from
||\tilde{v}_{q}||_{1}\tau^{c},\ ||\tilde{B}_{q}||_{1}\tau^{c}\leq 1
.

Lemma 3.2

(Classical Solutions with Forcing)
.

Let
\underline{r}=M-2m_{0}-k_{0}^{g}-7
, we have:

\begin{split}&||\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}+||\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1.\\
\end{split}

The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the parameters, but not on
a
.

We gather the estimates for:

(\tilde{\Delta}^{v},\ \tilde{\Delta}^{B},\ \tilde{\Delta}^{p})=(\tilde{v}_{\ell}-\tilde{v}_{\ell,j},\ \tilde{B}_{\ell}-\tilde{B}_{\ell,j},\ \tilde{p}_{\ell}-\tilde{p}_{\ell,j}),

in the following Lemma.

Lemma 3.3

(Stability Estimates)
.

Let
j\in\mathbb{Z}
,
\sigma=0,1,2
,
r\geq 0
integer and
\underline{r}=M-2m_{0}-k_{0}^{g}-7
. We have the following stability estimates:

(3.5a)

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}^{\sigma}(\tilde{v}_{\ell}-\tilde{v}_{\ell,j})||_{r+\alpha},\ ||\partial_{t}^{\sigma}(\tilde{B}_{\ell}-\tilde{B}_{\ell,j})||_{r+\alpha}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+\sigma+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-\sigma)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\tau^{c}\delta_{q},

(3.5b)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(\tilde{v}_{\ell}-\tilde{v}_{\ell,j})||_{r+\alpha},\ ||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(\tilde{B}_{\ell}-\tilde{B}_{\ell,j})||_{r+\alpha}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q},

(3.5c)

\displaystyle||\tilde{p}_{\ell}-\tilde{p}_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1,

(3.5d)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\left(\tilde{p}_{\ell}-\tilde{p}_{\ell,j}\right)||_{r+\alpha}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}(\ell\lambda_{q})^{m_{0}}\ell^{-2\alpha}\delta_{q}^{3/2}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1.

The implicit constants depend only on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

Note that there is a discrepancy in the Alfvén transport estimate for
\tilde{p}_{q}-\tilde{p}_{\ell}
and
\tilde{\Delta}^{p}
. This can be explained by the fact that the right-hand side in the stability equation has transport estimates inherited from those of the vector fields on which we have the expected one, namely

\partial_{t}\tilde{\Delta}^{\pm}+\tilde{z}_{\ell,j}^{\mp}\cdot\nabla\tilde{\Delta}^{\pm}+\tilde{\Delta}^{\mp}\cdot\nabla\tilde{z}_{\ell}^{\pm}+\nabla\tilde{\Delta}^{p}=\mathrm{div}\ [\tilde{R}_{\ell}^{c,\pm}]

where
\tilde{R}_{\ell}^{c,\pm}=\tilde{R}_{\ell}^{c}\pm[\tilde{M}_{\ell}]_{\times}
, see (
2.15
) for the details, while
\tilde{p}_{q}
sees the new fast-in-time oscillating right-hand side we introduced in the Galbrun stage, see Remark
11
.

We now gather the resulting estimates on the corrected vector fields and pressure.

Lemma 3.4

(Local Correction)
.

Let
\underline{r}=M-2m_{0}-k_{0}^{g}-7
and
j\in\mathbb{Z}
. The following bounds hold:

(3.6a)

\displaystyle||\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}||_{0},\ ||\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}||_{0}\leq C_{0},\ \ \ \ |\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}|\geq c_{0}\ \text{ everywhere on }\ \mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R},

(3.6b)

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}^{\sigma}\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}||_{r},\ ||\partial_{t}^{\sigma}\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+\sigma}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-\sigma)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ \sigma=0,1,2\ \text{ and }r\geq 0,

(3.6c)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}||_{r+\alpha},\ ||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 0,

(3.6d)

\displaystyle||\tilde{p}_{\ell,j}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1,

(3.6e)

\displaystyle||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\tilde{p}_{\ell,j}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ell^{-\alpha}(1/\tau^{a})\delta_{q+1}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1.

The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

3.2.
Mollification along the Alfvén Directions

Using the result of Section
3.1
, we now construct an operator regularising functions along the Alfvén directions. This kind of convolution along the flow is a generalisation of the construction first introduced by Isett in
[
27
]
to the case of multiple, commuting, space-time flows. This will be useful later to avoid a loss of transport derivatives on the slow coefficients, see (
3.21
).

Preliminaries.
Let
\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}
as in (
3.1
) but seen as vector fields
(\tilde{z}_{\ell,j}^{\pm},1)^{\top}
in
\mathbb{T}^{3}\times B_{\tau^{c}}(t_{j})\to\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}
. We associate to these the Lagrangian flow maps
\mathbb{X}^{j,\pm}
, that is, we solve:

\begin{cases}\partial_{s}\mathbb{X}^{j,\pm}_{s}(x,t)=\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(\mathbb{X}^{j,\pm}_{s}(x,t)),\\
\mathbb{X}^{j,\pm}_{0}(x,t)=(x,t).\end{cases}

The relation to the standard flow map is as follows. Let
X^{j,\pm}_{t,s}
solve:

\begin{cases}\partial_{s}X^{j,\pm}_{s,t}(x)=\tilde{z}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(X^{j,\pm}_{s,t}(x),s),\\
X^{j,\pm}_{t,t}=\mathrm{Id}\end{cases}

then, by the uniqueness of the Cauchy problem, we have:

(3.7)

\mathbb{X}^{j,\pm}_{s}(x,t)=(X^{j,\pm}_{t+s,t}(x),t+s).

This identity will be handy in a moment. Now, recall that given any two vector fields
v,w
with Lagrangian flow maps
X^{v},X^{w}
, we have:

[v,w]=0\iff X^{v}_{s}\circ X^{w}_{s^{\prime}}=X^{w}_{s^{\prime}}\circ X^{v}_{s}

see for example
[
32
, Chapter 10]
for a proof, taking
\partial_{s},\partial_{s^{\prime}}
and then letting
s=0,s^{\prime}=0
respectively one finds the identities:

(3.8)

\mathrm{D}X^{v}_{s}[w]=w(X^{v}_{s})\ \text{ and }\ \mathrm{D}X^{w}_{s^{\prime}}[v]=v(X^{w}_{s^{\prime}}),

we will apply these identities with
\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j},\ \mathbb{X}^{j,\pm}
as:

\frac{1}{2}[\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j},\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{-}_{\ell,j}]=\partial_{t}\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}+[\tilde{v}_{\ell,j},\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}]=0,\ [\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j},\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}]=0.

Mollification along the Alfvén directions.
Let
F
be a possibly tensor valued function:

F:\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}^{N}

with
\text{supp}_{t}F\subset B_{2/3\tau^{c}}(t_{j})
, and fix
\ell_{t}
with
\ell_{t}<\frac{1}{6}\tau^{c}
. We define the mollification along the Alfvén directions of
F
at scale
\ell_{t}
to be:

(F)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}:\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}^{N}

given by

(3.9)

\begin{split}(F)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}(x,t)=\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}F(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}(x,t))\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s,\end{split}

where
\rho\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R})
is a convolution kernel which we require to satisfy:

(3.10)

\int_{-1}^{1}\rho(s)\mathrm{d}s=1,\ \ \ \ \rho\geq 0,\ \ \ \ \text{supp}\ \rho\Subset(-1,1).

The fact that
\text{supp}_{t}F\subset B_{2/3\tau^{c}}(t_{j})
makes this object well-defined. To see this, we can argue as follows. The identity (
3.7
) shows that any point
t\in B_{2/3\tau^{c}}(t_{j})
gets translated by
\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}
at most of
2\ell_{t}
, but since
\ell_{t}<1/6\tau^{c}
we get that
B_{2/3\tau^{c}}(t_{j})\pm 2\ell_{t}\subset B_{\tau^{c}}(t_{j})
, here both flows are well defined and we have good control on their derivatives as
||\tilde{z}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}||_{1}\tau^{c}\leq 1
upon choosing
a
sufficiently large given
b,\ \beta
.

We gather the properties of this space-time regularisation in the following Lemma.

Lemma 3.5

(Mollification along the Alfvén directions)
.

Let
\underline{r}=M-2m_{0}-k_{0}^{g}-8
, fix
0<\ell_{t}<1/6\tau^{c}
and let
f
be a smooth function

f:\mathbb{T}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}\ \text{ with }\ \text{supp}_{t}f\subset B_{2/3\tau^{c}}(t_{j}).

The mollification along the Alfvén directions satisfies:

(3.11a)

\displaystyle||(f)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}||_{0}\leq||f||_{0},

(3.11b)

\displaystyle||(f)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}||f||_{1}+||f||_{r}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1,

(3.11c)

\displaystyle||(f)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}-f||_{0}\leq\ell_{t}\sum_{\sigma\in\{+,\ -\}}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{\sigma}f||_{0},

(3.11d)

\displaystyle||(f)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}-f||_{r}\lesssim\ell_{t}\sum_{\sigma\in\{+,\ -\}}(||\mathcal{A}_{\ell,j}^{\sigma}f||_{1}\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}+||\mathcal{A}_{\ell,j}^{\sigma}f||_{r})\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 1.

The implicit constants depend on
r
and all the parameters, but not on
a
. Moreover, the following properties hold:

(1)

Commutation:

\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(f)^{j}_{\ell_{t}}=(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}f)^{j}_{\ell_{t}}.

(2)

Integration by parts:

\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s=-\frac{1}{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\rho_{\ell_{t}}^{\prime}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s

and an analogous identity holds for
\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{-}_{\ell,j}
.

(3)

Support preservation:

\text{supp}_{x,t}(f)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}\subset B_{2(C_{0}+1)\ell_{t}}(\text{supp}_{x,t}\ f)

where
C_{0}
is as in (
1.16
).

In particular, the Lemma holds for
\ell_{t}=\tau^{a}
upon choosing
a
large enough.

Remark 13

(Commutation + IBP)
.

Combining the commutation and integration by parts properties allows one to control as many Alfvén transport derivatives as one desires upon paying
\ell_{t}^{-1}
for each additional one, see (
3.14
) for the explicit computation.

Remark 14
.

Although in the application we will use this operator only for scalar functions, the Lemma holds for the general vector-valued case.

Proof of Lemma
3.5
.

The proof is an adaptation of the corresponding one in
[
27
]
. The claims hold for
\ell_{t}=\tau^{a}
since by definition we have
\tau^{a}=\lambda_{q}^{-(b-1)\gamma_{a}}\tau^{c}
and given any
\gamma_{a}>0,\ b
we can choose
a
so large that
\tau^{a}<\frac{1}{6}\tau^{c}
.

Commutation property.
We first use the properties in (
3.8
), to compute:

\begin{split}\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})]&=(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})[(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})[\mathrm{D}_{x,t}\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}[\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}]]]\\
&=(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})[(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})[\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})]\\
&=(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})[(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}[\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}])(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})]\\
&=(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})[\mathcal{A}^{+}_{\ell,j}(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})]\\
&=(\mathrm{D}_{x,t}f)[\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}])(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\\
&=(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\end{split}

similar calculations hold for
\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{-}_{\ell,j}
and we conclude that:

(3.12)

\begin{split}&\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(f)^{j}_{\ell_{t}}=\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s=(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}f)^{j}_{\ell_{t}}.\end{split}

Integration by parts property.
We first compute:

(3.13)

\begin{split}&\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&=\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\partial_{s}[(f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})](\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&=\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\partial_{s}[(f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})]\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&=\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\underbrace{\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)}_{=0}|_{s=-\ell_{t}}^{s=+\ell_{t}}\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\\
&-\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}f(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})\partial_{s}(\rho_{\ell_{t}})(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&=-\frac{1}{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}f(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})\rho_{\ell_{t}}^{\prime}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s.\\
\end{split}

Commuting the flows, the same calculations can be done for
\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{-}_{\ell,j}
. In particular, we are free to take an additional Alfvén derivatives at a cost of
\ell_{t}^{-1}
. Indeed, we can use the identity (
3.13
) we just found and (
3.8
) again to compute:

(3.14)

\begin{split}\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\sigma}_{\ell,j}\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}(f)^{j}_{\ell_{t}}&=\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&=-\frac{1}{\ell_{t}}\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\sigma}_{\ell,j}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})]\rho_{\ell_{t}}^{\prime}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&=-\frac{1}{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\sigma}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})]\rho_{\ell_{t}}^{\prime}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\end{split}

where
\sigma\in\{+,\ -\}
. Iterating, one can take any number of Alfvén transport derivatives.

Support property.
We first note that thanks to the identity (
3.7
) for
|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}
we have:

\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T}^{3}\times B_{2/3\tau^{c}}(t_{j}))\subseteq\mathbb{T}^{3}\times B_{2/3\tau^{c}+\ell_{t}}(t_{j})

and thus for
|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t},\ |t-t_{j}|\leq 2/3\tau^{c}
we deduce:

(3.15)

\begin{split}|(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}|&\leq|(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t})(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})|+|\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}|\\
&\leq\left[\sup_{(x,t)\in\mathbb{T}^{3}\times B_{2/3\tau^{c}+\ell_{t}}(t_{j})}|\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}|\right]+|\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}|\\
&\leq|s|(||z^{+}_{\ell,j}||_{0}+1)+|s^{\prime}|(||z^{+}_{\ell,j}||_{0}+1)\\
&\leq 2(C_{0}+1)\ell_{t}\\
\end{split}

where we write
\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}
to mean the identity map
(x,t)\mapsto(x,t)
. We can now compute:

\begin{split}\text{supp}_{x,t}(f)^{j}_{\ell_{t}}&\subset\bigcup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}\text{supp}_{x,t}[(f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})]\\
&=\bigcup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})^{-1}(\text{supp}_{x,t}\ f)\\
&=\bigcup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})^{-1}\circ(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})^{-1}(\text{supp}_{x,t}\ f)\\
&=\bigcup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{-s^{\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{-s}(\text{supp}_{x,t}\ f)\\
&=\bigcup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}(\text{supp}_{x,t}\ f)\\
&\subset B_{2(C_{0}+1)\ell_{t}}(\text{supp}_{x,t}\ f)\end{split}

where we used that
\text{supp}_{t}f\subset B_{2/3\tau^{c}}(t_{j})
, and thus the bound in (
3.15
) holds. The support property follows.

Error computation.
Using the fact that the convolution kernel
\rho
has average one, we rewrite:

(3.16)

\begin{split}f_{\ell_{t}}-f&=\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\left[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}(x,t))-f(x,t)\right]\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&=\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\left[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}(x,t))-f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}(x,t))\right]\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&+\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\left[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}(x,t))-f(x,t)\right]\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
\end{split}

and we then express these differences as integrals of transport derivatives, the key point being that we want to compare the scale
\ell_{t}
with the transport estimates of
f
, namely

\begin{split}f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}(x,t))-f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}(x,t))&=\int_{0}^{s^{\prime}}\partial_{s^{\prime\prime}}[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime\prime}}(x,t))]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime\prime}\\
&=\int_{0}^{s^{\prime}}\partial_{s^{\prime\prime}}[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}(x,t))]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime\prime}\\
&=\int_{0}^{s}\partial_{s^{\prime\prime}}[f(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime\prime}})]\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}(x,t))]\mathrm{d}s^{\prime\prime}\\
&=\int_{0}^{s^{\prime}}(\mathcal{A}^{-}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime\prime}}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime\prime}.\\
\end{split}

We conclude that for any
|s|,\ |s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}
, we have:

(3.17)

\begin{split}||f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})-f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})||_{r}\leq&\ell_{t}||(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{-}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime\prime}})||_{r}\\
&\overset{r=0}{\leq}\ell_{t}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{-}f||_{0}\\
&\overset{r\geq 1}{\lesssim}\ell_{t}(||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{-}f||_{1}||\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime\prime}}||_{r}+||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{-}f||_{r}||\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime\prime}}||_{1}^{r})\\
&\lesssim\ell_{t}(||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{-}f||_{1}\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}+||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{-}f||_{r}).\end{split}

Similarly, one can rewrite:

f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}(x,t))-f(x,t)=\int_{0}^{s}(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}f)(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s^{\prime\prime}}(x,t))\mathrm{d}s^{\prime\prime}

and deduce the bound:

(3.18)

\begin{split}||f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})-f||_{r}&\overset{r=0}{\leq}\ell_{t}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{+}f||_{0}\\
&\overset{r\geq 1}{\lesssim}\ell_{t}(||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{+}f||_{1}\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}+||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}_{\ell,j}^{+}f||_{r}).\end{split}

We now use (
3.17
) and (
3.18
) to bound (
3.16
) and conclude that:

(3.19)

\begin{split}||(f)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}-f||_{r}&\overset{r=0}{\leq}\ell_{t}\sum_{\sigma\in\{+,\ -\}}||\mathcal{A}_{\ell,j}^{\sigma}f||_{0}\\
&\overset{r\geq 1}{\lesssim}\ell_{t}\sum_{\sigma\in\{+,\ -\}}(||\mathcal{A}_{\ell,j}^{\sigma}f||_{1}\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}+||\mathcal{A}_{\ell,j}^{\sigma}f||_{r}).\\
\end{split}

Derivative estimates.
We have:

(3.20)

\begin{split}||(f)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}||_{r}&\leq\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}\int_{-\ell_{t}}^{\ell_{t}}||f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})||_{r}\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s)\rho_{\ell_{t}}(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s\\
&\leq\sup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}||f(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})||_{r}\\
&\overset{r=0}{\leq}||f||_{0}\\
&\overset{r\geq 1}{\lesssim}\sup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}\left[||f||_{1}||\mathrm{D}(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})||_{r-1}+||f||_{r}||\mathrm{D}(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})||_{0}^{r}\right]\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}||f||_{1}+||f||_{r}.\end{split}

In the above, the bounds on the flows’ composition come from the following computation:

\begin{split}\sup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}||\mathrm{D}(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})||_{0}&\leq||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}||_{0}||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s^{\prime}}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}||_{0}+||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}||_{0}+||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s^{\prime}}-\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}||_{0}+|\mathrm{Id}_{x,t}|_{op}\\
&\leq 1+C\left(\ell_{t}^{2}\lambda_{q}^{2}\delta_{q}+\ell_{t}\lambda_{q}\delta_{q}^{1/2}\right)\\
&\leq 2\end{split}

where we used the estimates in Lemma
3.4
on
\tilde{z}^{\pm}_{\ell,j}
and Proposition
B.5
to deduce the ones on
\mathbb{X}^{\pm}
and the last inequality follows by choosing
a
sufficiently large given the other parameters and our definition of
\tau^{c}
in (
1.21
). For higher derivatives, by means of the composition estimates in Proposition
B.3
, we get:

\begin{split}\sup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}||\mathrm{D}(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})||_{r}&\lesssim\sup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}\left[||\mathrm{D}(\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s})\circ\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}})||_{r}||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}||_{0}+||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}||_{0}||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}||_{r}\right]\\
&\lesssim\sup_{|s|,|s^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{t}}\left[||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}||_{1}||\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}||_{r}+||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,+}_{s}||_{r}||\mathrm{D}\mathbb{X}^{j,-}_{s^{\prime}}||_{0}^{r}\right]+\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}](b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}.\end{split}

The implicit constants in the above statements depend on
r
and the other parameters, but not on
a
.
∎

3.3.
Update of the Decomposition

The goal of this section is to ‘update’ the geometric decomposition of Section
2.2
to take care of the additional Galbrun perturbation. We construct new charts
\tilde{\Psi}_{I}
adapted to
(\tilde{v}_{\ell,j},\tilde{B}_{\ell,j})
and obtain a new decomposition
\tilde{A}_{I}
. We then show that the difference
\tilde{A}_{I}-A_{I}
between the updated one and the old one is small, which corresponds to the flow error in
[
24
]
. This procedure is necessary to enhance the transport properties of the Nash LDF
\xi^{p}
, which will be constructed next.

We use the same space-time cut-off functions and cubes constructed in Section
2.2
, namely
\eta_{j},\theta_{j^{\prime}}
and
Q_{J}
. Proceeding as in Section
2.2
the commutation,
\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{-}_{\ell,j}=\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{-}_{\ell,j}\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{+}_{\ell,j}
the bounds in Lemma
3.4

and the results in Section
7
, namely Lemma
7.1
, allow us to construct charts
\tilde{\Psi}_{J}:Q_{J}\to\mathbb{R}^{3}
adapted to
(\tilde{v}_{\ell,j},\ \tilde{B}_{\ell,j})
. The conventions and notation used for the differential geometric objects can be found in Appendix
A
.

Lemma 3.6

(Chart Properties)
.

Let
J=(j,j^{\prime})
a chart label as in (
2.23
) and
\underline{r}=M-2m_{0}-k_{0}^{g}-8
. For any fixed
t\in B_{\tau^{c}}(t_{j})
the map:

\tilde{\Psi}_{J}(\cdot,t):B_{\tau^{c}}(x_{j^{\prime}})\to\mathbb{R}^{3}

is a diffeomorphism onto its image, with estimates:

\begin{split}&||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}-\mathrm{Id}||_{0},\ ||(\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J})^{-1}-\mathrm{Id}||_{0},\ ||\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}]-1||_{0}\lesssim\lambda_{q+1}^{-\alpha},\\
&||\partial_{t}^{\sigma}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}||_{r},||\partial_{t}^{\sigma}(\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J})^{-1}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+\sigma}\lambda_{q}^{[r+\sigma-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\ \text{ for }\ \sigma=0,1,2,\ r\geq 0\\
\end{split}

where the Hölder norms are understood to be taken on
Q_{J}
and the implicit constants depend on
r
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

Moreover, for any scalar function
\varphi:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}
,
k\in e_{3}^{\perp}
define

\varphi_{k,\lambda_{q+1}}(x)=\varphi(\lambda_{q+1}x\cdot k)
, which we regard as a function
\mathbb{R}^{3}\to\mathbb{R}
, and for any fixed unit vector
\nu
which we think of as a 1-form, we have:

\begin{cases}(\partial_{t}+\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}}^{1})\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{1*}\nu=0,\\
\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}}^{1}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{1*}\nu=0\\
\end{cases}\ \text{ and }\ \begin{cases}(\partial_{t}+\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}\cdot\nabla)\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{*}\varphi_{k,\lambda_{q+1}}=0,\\
\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}\cdot\nabla\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{*}\varphi_{k,\lambda_{q+1}}=0.\\
\end{cases}

In particular, if
(k,\nu,\zeta)\in\Lambda
from Lemma
2.5
, then

\xi=\frac{1}{\lambda_{q+1}}\mathrm{curl}[\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{1*}(\varphi_{k}\nu)]=\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{2*}(\varphi_{k,\lambda_{q+1}}^{\prime}\zeta)

is a well-defined vector field on
Q_{J}
satisfying:

(\partial_{t}+\mathcal{L}_{\tilde{v}_{\ell,j}})\xi=0,\ \mathcal{L}_{\tilde{B}_{\ell,j}}\xi=0,\ \mathrm{div}\ \xi=0.

Finally, given any positive integer
\bar{N}
, there exists
a
sufficiently large so that:

||\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{1*}(\varphi_{\lambda,k}\nu)||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q+1}^{r}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq\bar{N},

where the implicit constant depends only on
r
but not on
C_{0},\ a
and the other parameters.

The Lemma is just a reformulation of
7.1
in the current notation. For the final additional estimate, see (
3.40
) in the proof of Lemma
3.10
.

The key takeaway here is that the geometric constraint in Lemma
2.5
pairs with the fact that the charts map
B_{\ell,j}
to a vector parallel to
e_{3}
. We will use this in the construction of the Nash LDF, see (
3.33
).

We can now update
A_{I}
to
\tilde{A}_{I}
and
a_{I}
to
\tilde{a}_{I}
. A key difference here compared to the construction in the Galbrun stage is that we also mollify along the Alfvén direction the slow coefficients by means of the operator
(\dots)_{\ell_{t}}^{j}
defined in (
3.9
). Namely, for
I=(\zeta,J)
, we set:

(3.21)

\begin{split}\tilde{a}_{I}&=\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\left(\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{T})\right)^{I_{t}}_{\ell_{t}},\\
\tilde{A}_{I}&=\delta_{q+1}\tilde{a}_{I}^{2}\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{2*}\zeta\otimes\tilde{\Psi}_{J}^{2*}\zeta.\\
\end{split}

In the classical constructions, e.g.
[
27
]
one mollifies only the Reynolds stress; this is not sufficient because we can provide transport estimates for the charts up to second-order, but in the transport part of the new Reynolds stress, we will need a third-order one (see
T_{1}
in the proof of Lemma
3.18
). We now collect all the bounds relative to the slow coefficients. We remark that, despite the mollification along the Alfvén directions, the coefficients associated with charts of the same type still have disjoint supports.

Lemma 3.7

(Estimates on the Decomposition)
.

Let
p
be the type map in (
2.28
) and
\underline{r}=M-2m_{0}-k_{0}^{g}-8
. For any
I=(\zeta,j,j^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}
the slow coefficients
\tilde{a}_{I}
satisfy:

||\tilde{a}_{I}||_{0}\lesssim\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}

where the implicit constant is independent of
C_{0}
. Moreover,

\begin{split}&||\partial_{t}^{j}\tilde{a}_{I}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ j=0,1,2\ \text{ and }\ r\geq 0,\\
&||(\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,I})^{j+1}\tilde{a}_{I}||_{r}\lesssim 1/\tau^{c}\ell_{t}^{-j}\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ r,j\geq 0\\
\end{split}

and we have stability estimates:

(3.22)

\begin{split}&||\tilde{a}_{I}-a_{I}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}[\ell_{t}/\tau^{c}+\mathcal{T}_{g}]\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ r\geq 0,\\
&||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}(\tilde{a}_{I}-a_{I})||_{r}\lesssim 1/\tau^{c}\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ 0\leq r\leq N-m_{0}\\
\end{split}

where the implicit constants depend on
r,\ j
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

Finally, we have the following support localisation properties:

(3.23)

\begin{split}&\text{supp}_{x,t}\tilde{a}_{I}\Subset B_{\tau^{c}}(x_{j^{\prime}})\times B_{\tau^{c}}(t_{j}),\\
&\text{supp}_{x,t}\tilde{a}_{I}\subset B_{C\ell_{t}}(\text{supp}_{x,t}R_{\ell}),\\
&p(I_{1})=p(I_{2})\Longrightarrow\text{supp}_{x,t}\tilde{a}_{I_{1}}\cap\text{supp}_{x,t}\tilde{a}_{I_{2}}=\emptyset\end{split}

for any
I,\ I_{1},\ I_{2}\in\mathcal{I}
and some constant
C
depending on the parameters but not on
a
.

Remark 15
.

The fact that we don’t lose one good derivative in the transport bound comes from the fact that we lost one already in the construction of the chart, compared to the ones we have on the vector fields, but note that we lose one in the stability estimate (
3.22
) i.e. two on the vector fields, as per Lemma
7.2
.

Remark 16
.

From the
j
th-order Alfvén transport bound arguing as in (
3.31
), follows that:

||\partial_{t}^{j}(\mathcal{A}^{\pm}_{\ell,I})\tilde{a}_{I}||_{r}\lesssim 1/\tau^{c}\lambda_{q}^{r+j}\lambda_{q}^{[r+j-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ j=0,1,2\ \text{ and }\ r\geq 0.

This will be needed later.

Proof of Lemma
3.7
.

We will apply the properties of the mollification along the Alfvén directions studied in Section
3.2
, see Lemma
3.5
, and the estimates in Lemmas
3.1
and
3.6
. In what follows, abusing notation, we always index
I
, even if the object in question does not depend on all its components. Moreover, the
C^{0}_{t}C^{r}_{x}
norms are thought on the space-time domain
Q_{I}
of the chart.

Pure derivative estimates.
We first note that for
a
sufficiently large
\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\in B_{1/2}(\mathrm{Id})
, moreover, for an implicit constant which depends on
r
we have:

(3.24)

||\det[\cdots]^{-2}|_{B_{1/2}(\mathrm{Id})}||_{r}\lesssim 1

the composition estimates in Proposition
B.3
for
r\geq 1
give:

\begin{split}||\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}||_{r}\lesssim||\det[\cdots]^{-2}|_{B_{1/2}(\mathrm{Id})}||_{1}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||_{r}+||\det[\cdots]^{-2}|_{B_{1/2}(\mathrm{Id})}||_{r}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||_{1}^{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\end{split}

and we deduce:

\begin{split}||\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T}||_{r}&\lesssim||\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}||_{r}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||^{2}_{0}||\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}||_{0}\\
&+||\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}||_{0}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||^{2}_{0}||\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}||_{r}\\
&+||\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}||_{0}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||_{0}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||_{r}||\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}||_{0}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}},\end{split}

from (
3.20
) we conclude:

(3.25)

\begin{split}||\tilde{a}_{I}||_{r}&=||\left(\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})\right)^{I_{t}}_{\ell_{t}}||_{r}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&\overset{r=0}{\leq}||\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})||_{0}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&\leq||\gamma_{\zeta}|_{B_{1/2}(\mathrm{Id})}||_{0}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\lesssim\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&\overset{r\geq 1}{\lesssim}||\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})||_{1}\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&+||\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})||_{r}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\end{split}

where the implicit constant for
r\geq 0
depends on
r
and all the other parameters. Note, however, that the bound for
r=0
depends only on
\beta,\ b
but not on
C_{0}
and the other parameters.

Alfvén transport estimates.
From (
7.7
) in Remark
27
we have that:

\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]=0.

The transport estimates for the charts follow from the Lie-transport properties in Lemma
7.1
, namely

\begin{split}&\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}[B_{\ell,I}]=ce_{3}\Longrightarrow 0=\mathrm{D}(\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}[B_{\ell,I}])=\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\mathrm{D}B_{\ell,I}+B_{\ell,I}\cdot\nabla\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I},\\
&\partial_{t}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}+\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}[v_{\ell,I}]=0\Longrightarrow 0=\mathrm{D}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}+\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}[v_{\ell,I}])=(\partial_{t}+v_{\ell,I}\cdot\nabla)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}+\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\mathrm{D}v_{\ell,I}\end{split}

and thus since
z^{\pm}_{\ell,I}=v_{\ell,I}\pm B_{\ell,I}
combination of the two gives:

\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}=(\partial_{t}+z^{\pm}_{\ell,I})\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}=\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\mathrm{D}z^{\pm}_{\ell,I},

by taking the transpose, we also see that:

\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}(\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I})^{\top}=(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I})^{\top}=(\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\mathrm{D}z^{\pm}_{\ell,I})^{\top}=(\mathrm{D}z^{\pm}_{\ell,I})^{\top}(\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I})^{\top}

and from the bounds in
3.6
and
3.4
we deduce:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||_{r},\ ||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}(\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I})^{\top}||_{r}&\lesssim||z^{\pm}_{\ell,I}||_{r+1}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||_{0}+||z^{\pm}_{\ell,I}||_{1}||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r+1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q}^{1/2}.\\
\end{split}

With this at hand and the bounds in Lemmas
3.6
,
2.1
and (
2.24
), we first obtain:

(3.26)

\begin{split}&||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\left[\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})\right]||_{r}\\
&\lesssim||(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\chi_{I})\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\gamma_{\zeta}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T}||_{r}\\
&+||\chi_{I}\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\gamma_{\zeta}(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I})\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T}||_{r}\\
&+||\chi_{I}\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\gamma_{\zeta}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T}||_{r}\\
&+||\chi_{I}\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\gamma_{\zeta}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})||_{r}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}1/\tau^{c}\\
\end{split}

and conclude, using (
3.12
) to commute the mollification along the flow and the transport operator and (
3.20
) to compute the
C^{r}
norm, that:

\begin{split}||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\tilde{a}_{I}||_{r}&=||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\left(\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})\right)^{I_{t}}_{\ell_{t}}||_{r}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&=||\left(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\left[\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})\right]\right)^{I_{t}}_{\ell_{t}}||_{r}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\left[\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})\right]||_{1}\lambda_{q}^{r-1}\lambda_{q}^{[r-1-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&+||\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I}\left[\chi_{I}\gamma_{\zeta}(\det[\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}]^{-2}\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}\left(\mathrm{Id}-R_{\ell}/\delta_{q+1}\right)\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}^{T})\right]||_{r}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}1/\tau^{c}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}.\end{split}

We can move any additional derivative to the kernel in the mollification along the flow, see (
3.13
), and we conclude:

(3.27)

||(\mathcal{\tilde{A}}^{\pm}_{\ell,I})^{j+1}\tilde{a}_{I}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-\underline{r}]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}1/\tau^{c}\ell_{t}^{-j}\delta_{q+1}^{1/2}\ \text{ for }\ r,j\geq 0.

The implicit constant depends on
r,j
and all the other parameters, but not on
a
.

Stability estimates.
According to Lemma
7.2
we have:

||\mathrm{D}\tilde{\Psi}_{I}-\mathrm{D}\Psi_{I}||_{r}\lesssim\lambda_{q}^{r}\lambda_{q}^{[r-(\underline{r}-1)]^{+}(b-1)\gamma_{\ell}}\mathcal{T}_{g},

we now split:
